##  **PALMESTER |**  
**PLANTERA**  
mct
### Empowering Your Health Journey
PALMESTER | PLANTERA Medium-Chain Triglycerides (MCTs) derived from renewable natural sources. It has a higher absorption in the human body compared to long-chain triglycerides (LCTs).
It functions as an energy booster and helps weight management. It enhances infant nutrition and serves as a brain fuel source to promote cognitive health. It is colourless, odourless & tasteless, which makes it a valuable solution for the flavour industry and can be used as food additives into different food & beverage applications, or consumed directly as a dietary supplement.
Discover the power of PALMESTER | PLANTERA MCT. Meet us at Vitafoods Asia to learn more!

### Discover your potential with KLK OLEO
KLK OLEO’s footprint are in 9 different countries across the world with a workforce surpassing 3,000 employees globally. As we continue to expand our business, we believe that employees are of paramount importance to our long-term business sustainability and growth; thus, we continuously seek talents to join our existing workforce.
A career with KLK OLEO means you will be part of something genuinely special. Embracing the Company’s Vision and Values, employees fulfil our Mission to improve more lives and deliver stronger, more effective business results. Through our employee development and competencies framework, we believe in equipping employees with the right tools for their growth and to support the business continuity plan.
We are looking for talents with passion, energy, enthusiasm; and more importantly, aligned to our Core Values of _**T** eamwork, **H** umility, **R** esults, **I** ntegrity, **I** nnovation and **L** oyalty._ Join us, be part of our team, and we will unlock your full potential.

# About Us
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » [Company](https://www.klkoleo.com/company/) » About Us
  

  

  

### About KLK OLEO
KLK OLEO is one of the world’s leading oleochemical producers committed to delivering excellence in the global marketplace.
Our integrated oleochemicals complexes located in key sourcing and supply markets (Malaysia, Indonesia, China and Europe) produce a wide range of high quality sustainable oleochemical products from natural renewable raw materials.
KLK OLEO is the oleochemicals manufacturing division of [Kuala Lumpur Kepong Berhad (KLK)](https://www.klk.com.my/), a leading international plantations group listed on the Main Market of Bursa Malaysia Securities Berhad. Strategically integrated with our upstream plantations parent, KLK OLEO can extract synergies and focus on developing sustainable solutions and reliable supply for our customers. At KLK OLEO, sustainability is integral to our business. We believe that everything we do impacts the entire ecosystem, going beyond just people. Together with our customers and partners, we have the power to create more sustainable products and solutions.
KLK OLEO’s production portfolio ranges from basic oleochemical products, such as Fatty Acids, Glycerine, Fatty Alcohols and Fatty Esters, all the way down the spectrum to specialties, such as Methyl Ester Sulphonates (MES), Surfactants and Phytonutrients. Our products are used in diverse end-use applications, including beauty & personal care, home care, industries & institutional (I&I) cleaning, life science, food & nutrition, lubricants, polymers and many more.
We strongly believe in innovation and have a strong focus on research and development. Creating new downstream businesses is central to our strategy of maximising the integrated value chain. KLK OLEO’s approach combines research and industry focus to develop innovative and sustainable products for customers and partners, ensuring performance, quality, and security of supply.
We are proud to be a part of the global oleochemical industry. By focusing on sustainability, quality, operation excellence, people and ethical practices, our vision is growing to be the most trusted global partner in oleo-based products and solutions, thus enriching lives in a sustainable manner every day.
  

  

  

![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/2025-RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
  

  

  
  

  

  

### Our Vision
Growing to be the most trusted global partner in oleo-based products and solutions, thus enriching lives in a sustainable manner every day.
  

### Our Mission
  * **Sustainability**  
Commit to deliver positive impact to climate and people in every possible way.
  * **Quality**  
Consistent delivery of competitive high quality products and solutions that are focused on meeting and exceeding customer expectations.
  * **Operational Excellence**  
Value addition through commitment to the highest standards of operational excellence driven by a culture of continuous improvement and innovation.
  * **People**  
Cultivating a team that values and develops people of all backgrounds through empowerment and recognition.
  * **Ethical Practices**  
Values built on the legacy of ethical practices embraced by its founder, committed to operate responsibly and with integrity.

  

  

  
  

  

  

### Core Values _T_._H_._R_._I_._I_._L_
  * ###### Teamwork
Going beyond geographical, divisional and functional boundaries to achieve common goals. Collaboration is at the heart of all we do.
  * ###### Humility
Having an open mind to recognise the strength of others and willingness to stretch ourselves and grow.
  * ###### Results
Committed to exceptional execution and excellence to make a difference in everything we do.
  * ###### Integrity
Ethical leadership with a strong moral compass that inspires every individual to do the right thing.
  * ###### Innovation
Levelling up through new ideas and continuous improvement to consistently exceed expectations.
  * ###### Loyalty
Committed to building a better future together, with mutual trust and care while upholding the Company’s interest as our own.

# Corporate Responsibility
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Corporate Responsibility
### Values Matter, Society Matters
We are a socially responsible corporate citizen, committed to the communities around where we operate in to deliver a sustainable future.
We believe that corporations are platforms for social change, contributing time and resources to build a better tomorrow.
In achieving these goals, four core elements – Education, Community, Environment and Employee are established as pillars of the Corporate Responsibility initiative. The Corporate Responsibility programmes are designed to help the employees build teamwork, establish engagement, and to nurture them to become responsible corporate citizens.
### Our Commitments
Education
Community
Environment
Employees
![Employees](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/cr-employeest-01.jpg)

##  Employees
######  Health and Wellbeing 
Employees are one of our greatest assets and their health and wellbeing remain our top priority. We aim to support our employees to be the best they can be by providing them an environment and opportunities that encourage and enable them to lead a healthy lifestyle.

##  Education
######  Empowerment Through Education 
We believe that education is an important catalyst for positive change and transform the future of the community. As such, we seek to create appropriate education inroads and pathways in the areas where we operate for people and communities who may not be fortunate enough to have access to such opportunities.
![Community](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/cr-communityt-01.jpg)

##  Community
######  Care for People 
At KLK, our values and the culture of caring continues through the generations and is embedded in our corporate DNA. In the spirit of caring and sharing, we reach out and engage the less fortunate in various communities. We will endeavor to improve the livelihoods of the communities in areas where we operate.
![Environment](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/cr-environment-01.jpg)

##  Environment
######  Harmony with Nature 
We place great importance on the protection and preservation of the environment and this is reflected in our core business practices and policies. We strongly believe that our livelihoods depend on a healthier, more livable world for us and our future generations.
![Employees](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/cr-employeest-01.jpg)

##  Employees

Employees are one of our greatest assets and their health and wellbeing remain our top priority. We aim to support our employees to be the best they can be by providing them an environment and opportunities that encourage and enable them to lead a healthy lifestyle.

#### History & Milestones - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/history-milestones/

# History & Milestones
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » History & Milestones
![1906](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-1906-square.jpg)
##  1906
The Kuala Lumpur Rubber Company, Limited (KLR) was founded in London to oversee 600 ha. plantations (rubber and coffee) in Malaya (now Malaysia). 
![1960](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/1960-1.jpg)
##  1960
The Group started to plant oil palm in Fraser Estate.
![1973](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/1973.jpg)
##  1973
1st October 1973: Kuala Lumpur Kepong Berhad (KLK) was incorporated in Malaysia and listed publicly on Kuala Lumpur, Singapore and London Stock Exchanges.
![KLK Founder](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/1991-scaled-1.jpg)
##  KLK Founder
The late Tan Sri Dato’ Seri Lee Loy Seng
![1991](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/1991-Founder.png)
##  1991
KLK’s Chief Executive Officer, Tan Sri Dato’ Seri Lee Oi Hian invested in resource-based downstream manufacturing with the opening of its first oleochemical plant, [Palm-Oleo](https://www.klkoleo.com/palm-oleo-sdn-bhd/) in Rawang.  
The birth of KLK OLEO.
![1994](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-1991-square.jpg)
##  1994
Official Opening of [Palm-Oleo](https://www.klkoleo.com/palm-oleo-sdn-bhd/), officiated by Y.A.B. Dato Seri Dr. Mahathir Mohamad.
![1996](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-1996-square.jpg)
##  1996
Launching of[ Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/palmosalt-soap-download/palmosalt-soap-noodles/) at KSP Manufacturing.
![1997](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-1997-square.jpg)
##  1997
Introduction of [Ethylene Bis-Stearamide](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmowax-fatty-acid-bis-amides/) and [Alkanolamides](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmocol-alkanolamides/palmocol-alkanolamides/) at Palmamide.
![2004](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-2004-square.jpg)
##  2004
Greenfield expansion at [Zhangjiagang, China](https://www.klkoleo.com/taiko-palm-oleo-zhangjiagang-co-ltd/), manufacturer of [Fatty Acids](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-fatty-acids/), [Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-glycerine/), [Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/palmosalt-soap-download/palmosalt-soap-noodles/) and[ Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/products/esters/).
![2005](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/2005-Davos.png)
##  2005
Investing in [Davos Life Science Ptd Ltd](https://www.klkoleo.com/davoslife/) in Singapore, manufacturer of phytonutrient from palm fruits.
![2005](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/2005-Stolthaven.png)
##  2005
Investing in [Stolthaven (Westport)](https://www.klkoleo.com/stolthavenwestport/) in Malaysia, a storage terminal operator, to optimise the oleochemical supply chain.
![2006](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/2006-Oleomas.png)
##  2006
Introduction of the first fully Malaysian-owned [Fatty Alcohols](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmerol-fatty-alcohols/) plant at[ KL-Kepong Oleomas](https://www.klkoleo.com/kl-kepong-oleomas-sdn-bhd/), Westport, Port Klang.
![2006](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/MegaMenu-Aboutus-200-01.jpg)
##  2006
Launching of corporate brand “KLK OLEO”, assembling all oleochemical operations under ONE IDENTITY.
![2007](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-2007-02-square.jpg)
##  2007
Acquisition of Uniqema Malaysia – renamed as [Palm-Oleo (Klang)](https://www.klkoleo.com/palm-oleo-klang-sdn-bhd/), manufacturer of [Fatty Acids](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-fatty-acids/), [Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-glycerine/), [Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/products/esters/) and [Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/palmosalt-soap-download/palmosalt-soap-noodles/).
![2007](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-2007-01-square.jpg)
##  2007
Acquisition of [Dr. W.Kolb Holding](https://www.kolb.ch/company/#delden), a large independent alkoxylator and a significant manufacturer and distributor of [Nonionic Surfactants](https://www.klkoleo.com/products/nonionic-surfactants/) and [Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/products/esters/).
![2009](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/2009.png)
##  2009
Commissioning of Methyl Esters plant at KL-Kepong Oleomas.  
New Oleo-based [Methyl Ester Sulphonate (MES)](https://www.klkoleo.com/?r3d=palmfonate-methyl-ester-sulphonate) introduced at [KL-Kepong Oleomas](https://www.klkoleo.com/kl-kepong-oleomas-sdn-bhd/) as an environment-friendly surfactant.

##  2009
Acquisition of Zoop’s biodiesel plant (renamed as [KLK Bioenergy](https://www.klkoleo.com/klk-bioenergy-sdn-bhd/)).
![2010](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-2010-square.jpg)
##  2010
Expansion of KLK OLEO’s footprint in Europe through the acquisition of Croda Emmerich, Germany (renamed as [KLK Emmerich](https://www.klkoleo.com/klkemmerich/)).
![2011](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/milestone-2011-square.jpg)
##  2011
Construction of [KLK Dumai](https://www.klkoleo.com/pt-klk-dumai/) facility in Indonesia, further added to the Group’s overall [Fatty Acids](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-fatty-acids/) and[ Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-glycerine/) production capability.
![2012](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/Davoslife-NOV-2015-1LR.jpg)
##  2012
Relocation of[ Davos Life Science](https://www.klkoleo.com/davoslife/)’s operation at Singapore to Westport, Malaysia, and with expansion of production capacity.
1 [2](https://www.klkoleo.com/history-milestones/page/2/#wphtsp-history-1) [Next »](https://www.klkoleo.com/history-milestones/page/2/#wphtsp-history-1)

#### Karier - KLK OLEO

# Karier
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Karier
[**ENGLISH**](https://www.klkoleo.com/careers/) | [**BAHASA INDONESIA**](https://www.klkoleo.com/karier/)
###  KLK OLEO  Secara Singkat
KLK OLEO adalah salah satu produsen oleokimia terkemuka di dunia yang berkomitmen untuk memberikan produk terbaik di pasar global. Kawasan oleokimia kami yang terintegrasi berlokasi di pasar yang memiliki sumber daya dan pemasok utama (Malaysia, Indonesia, Tiongkok, Amerika dan Eropa) dalam membuat berbagai macam produk oleokimia dengan kualitas tinggi dan berkelanjutan dari bahan baku alami yang terbarukan.
KLK OLEO adalah bagian dari Kuala Lumpur Kepong Berhad (“KLK”), sebuah kelompok perkebunan internasional yang terdaftar di Pasar Utama Bursa Malaysia Securities Berhad. Berlokasi secara strategis dan terintegrasi dengan induk perkebunan kami, KLK OLEO mampu mengekstraksi sinergi dan berfokus pada solusi pembangunan berkelanjutan serta menjadi produsen yang dapat diandalkan oleh pelanggan kami.
Portofolio produksi KLK OLEO berkisar dari produk oleokimia dasar, _fatty acids, glycerine, fatty alcohols dan fatty esters_ , hingga produk khusus, seperti _methyl ester sulphonate (MES), surfactants dan phytonutrients_. Produk kami digunakan dalam berbagai macam aplikasi penggunaan akhir, termasuk perawatan kesehatan rumah dan pribadi, kosmetik & perlengkapan mandi, makanan, rasa & wewangian, pelumas, polimer, dan bahan kimia industri. Didukung oleh keyakinan yang kuat dalam berinovasi dan kekuatan budaya R&D, penciptaan hilirisasi bisnis baru terus menjadi landasan strategi kami untuk memanfaatkan rantai nilai terintegrasi.
* * *
Jaringan KLK OLEO telah berada di 9 negara berbeda di seluruh dunia dengan tenaga kerja lebih dari 3.000 karyawan secara global. Seiring dengan berkembangnya bisnis kami, maka kami percaya bahwa karyawan adalah aset terpenting bagi keberlanjutan dan pertumbuhan bisnis jangka panjang kami; oleh karena itu, kami terus mencari talenta terbaik untuk bergabung menjadi tenaga kerja di perusahaan kami.
Berkarier bersama KLK OLEO berarti Anda menjadi bagian dari hal yang istimewa di perusahaan kami. Dengan mengusung Visi dan Nilai Perusahaan, karyawan melaksanakan Misi kami untuk meningkatkan taraf hidup dan memberikan hasil bisnis yang lebih kuat dan efektif. Melalui kerangka kerja pengembangan dan kompetensi karyawan, kami yakin dapat meningkatkan kinerja karyawan dengan cara terbaik dan mendukung kesinambungan rencana bisnis.
Kami mencari talenta yang memiliki semangat, energi, antusiasme; dan yang lebih penting, selaras dengan Nilai Utama kami yang terdiri dari _**T** eamwork, **H** umility, **R** esults, **I** ntegrity, **I** nnovation and **L** oyalty._ Bergabunglah dan menjadi bagian dari tim kami, dan kami akan membantu mengembangkan potensi Anda secara penuh.
Lihat peluang kerja bersama KLK OLEO melalui saluran berikut:  

  * [LinkedIn](https://www.linkedin.com/company/klkberhad)
  * talents@klkoleo.com

#### KL-Kepong Oleomas Sdn. Bhd. - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/kl-kepong-oleomas-sdn-bhd/

# KL-Kepong Oleomas Sdn. Bhd.
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » KL-Kepong Oleomas Sdn. Bhd.
As one the world’s largest producers of natural Fatty Alcohols, KL-Kepong Oleomas (“KLKOM”) produces a wide variety of high quality oleochemical products.
KLKOM’s key products are [PALMEROL Fatty Alcohols](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmerol-fatty-alcohols/), [PALMERE Fractionated Methyl Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmere-methyl-esters/) and [PALMFONATE Methyl Ester Sulphonates (MES)](https://www.klkoleo.com/?r3d=palmfonate-methyl-ester-sulphonate). They are 100% vegetable oil based and produced from readily renewable and sustainable natural resources.
[PALMEROL Fatty Alcohols](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmerol-fatty-alcohols/) are widely used as emulsifying, dispersing, wetting agents or surfactants. C12 to C18 alcohols provide starting materials for all surfactant types. [PALMERE Fractionated Methyl Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmere-methyl-esters/) are mainly employed as intermediates for chemical synthesis and used as fuel bio-additives. [PALMFONATE MES](https://www.klkoleo.com/?r3d=palmfonate-methyl-ester-sulphonate) is the main ingredient in powder or liquid detergents. The newly launched [SYMPARE MES](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/sympare-methyl-ester-sulphonates/) is a sulphate-free cleaning agent for use in the personal care categories. [SYMPARE MES](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/sympare-methyl-ester-sulphonates/) is listed in the CleanGredients database as it meets the EPA’s Safer Choice Standard.
In 2021, KLKOM has successfully added [TENSOPOL Sodium Lauryl Sulphate (“SLS”)](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/tensopol-alcohol-sulphates/) and [TENSAGEX Sodium Lauryl Ether Sulphate (“SLES”)](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/tensagex-alcohol-ether-sulphates/) into their product portfolio as primary surfactants home and personal care industries.
KLKOM’s manufacturing plant is strategically located at Westport, Malaysia, allowing direct pipeline access from the plant to the port, for fast and effective supply and distribution to international markets.
![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/12/KL-Kepong-Oleomas-scaled.jpg)

#### KLK Bioenergy Sdn. Bhd. - KLK OLEO

# KLK Bioenergy Sdn. Bhd.
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » KLK Bioenergy Sdn. Bhd.
The Group has added another manufacturing facility via KLK Oleomas’ acquisition of Biodiesel plant Zoop Sdn. Bhd. on 6th February 2009. Renamed KLK Bioenergy Sdn. Bhd., the Biodiesel plant’s primary industry is biodiesel production with a plant capacity of 100,000mt/annum. This biodiesel product has been given the trade name of [PALMERGY](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmergy-biodiesels/). Zoop was incorporated in Malaysia on 24 September 2003 and holds a manufacturing license for the manufacture of biodiesel. The main nature of its business is biofuels production and supply services and general trading in[ methyl esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmere-methyl-esters/).

# KLK OLEO Americas Inc.
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » KLK OLEO Americas Inc.
KLK OLEO has expanded its business footprint to the Americas in 2020 with a new sales office set up at Woodbury, NY, USA. KLK OLEO Americas (KLKOA) will oversee the sales and marketing in the United States, Canada and South America. And continuously maintain and build customer relationships for the KLK OLEO group product portfolio.
KLKOA will create a local distribution network through storage locations across the US for packed and bulk materials to serve the customers in the Americas region with just-in-time deliveries.

KLK EMMERICH GmbH is Europe’s leading oleochemical manufacturer, drawing on more than a century of experience in oleochemical production.
We provide a broad and versatile product portfolio that plays a key role in a wide range of industrial applications.
Based in Emmerich am Rhein and Düsseldorf, KLK EMMERICH GmbH specialises in the manufacture of essential oleochemical ingredients, including a comprehensive range of Fatty Acids and Glycerine. In addition, we offer high-value specialty chemicals such as Isostearic Acids, Monomer, Dimer and Trimer Acids, MCT oils, and Triacetin.
Our brands in the global arena:
  * **[PALMERA](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmera-fatty-acids/)**
  * **[PALMESTER](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/)**
  * **[PLANTERA](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/plantera-fatty-acids/)**
  * **[EDENOR](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/edenor-triacetin/)**
  * **[ENHEX](https://www.klkoleo.com/lifescience/)**

KLK EMMERICH GmbH is deeply committed to providing renewable, bio-based ingredients for the food / feed, personal care, home care, industrial and life science industries.
Our portfolio is further enhanced by Fatty Alcohols, Esters, Anionic Surfactants, and Amides supplied by the KLK OLEO Group.
As a vertically integrated company and a founding member of the RSPO, KLK OLEO guarantees a transparent and responsible supply chain.
Supported by strong technical expertise and a continuously expanding global presence, we strive to consistently surpass our customers’ expectations.

Governance & Policies at KLK Temix 
  * KLK Temix General Conditions of Sale – [**English**](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/KLK-Temix-General-Conditions-of-Sale-English.pdf)
  * KLK Temix Code of Ethics – [**English**](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/KLK-TEmix-Code-of-Ethics-English_June-2018.pdf)
  * KLK Temix Whistleblowing Policy – [**Italian**](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/KLK-Temix-Whistleblowing-Policy-Italian_December-2023.pdf)
  * KLK Temix Quality, Environmental, And Food Safety Policy – [**English**](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/KLK-Temix-Quality-Environmental-And-Food-Safety-Policy-English_October-2025.pdf)

KLK Temix SpA is part of KLK OLEO, the oleochemical division of Kuala Lumpur Kepong Berhad (KLK). Based in Italy, we bring together a rich heritage of tradition and values with a wide ranging product portfolio and a steadfast commitment to customer satisfaction. This unique combination allows us to provide tailored solutions that meet the specific needs of diverse industries and markets. As a global leader in oleochemicals with more than a century of experience, KLK OLEO is dedicated to providing innovative and sustainable solutions in line with worldwide trends and customer demands.
KLK Temix is specialised in the production and trading of oleochemicals and chemicals such as Esters, Fatty Alcohols, Waxes and Specialties mainly derived from renewable resources for a wide range of applications, including Lubricants, Beauty & Personal Care and Polymers.
Our extensive product portfolio is sold under different brand names:
  * **[TEMEST Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/temest-esters/)**
  * **[PLANTERA Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/plantera-esters/)**
  * **[PALMESTER Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/)**
  * A diversified portfolio of **[Traded Products](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/traded-products/)** tailored to specific market needs.

  
This reflects our ongoing commitment to enhancing brand identity and ensuring greater consistency between formulation, market positioning and communication.
Founded in 2014 through the merger of Oleochimica Italia and Temix International, we have rapidly established ourselves as a key player in the Italian and European markets. Our operations are rooted in a deep respect for the environment, ethics and sustainability. We value human resources, investing in the professional development of our employees and prioritising technological advances to improve performance and ensure that our processes and products are both reliable and environmentally friendly.
As a member of key industry associations such as Federchimica, Confindustria Emilia Romagna, AssICC and APAG, we are actively involved in shaping the future of the chemical and oleochemical industries.

# KLK Tensachem S.A
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » KLK Tensachem S.A
KLK Tensachem – Your Trusted Partner in Surfactant Solutions.
Located in Ougrée (Liège), Belgium, KLK Tensachem is a leading European manufacturer of high-performance anionic surfactants, proudly serving a wide range of global industries.
With decades of experience and technical expertise, KLK Tensachem offers a comprehensive portfolio of products, including:
[TENSAGEX (Alcohol Ether Sulphates)](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/tensagex-alcohol-ether-sulphates/)  
[TENSARYL (Linear Alkylbenzene Sulphonates & Sulphonic Acids)](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/tensaryl-linear-alkylbenzene-sulphonates-sulphonic-acids/)  
[TENSOMILD (Sulphosuccinates)](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/tensomild-sulphosuccinates/)  
[TENSOPOL (Alcohol Sulphates)](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/tensopol-alcohol-sulphates/)
These surfactants are available in various forms — liquid (concentrated and diluted), powder, and needle — to meet the diverse needs of our customers across segments such as Home Care, Industrial & Institutional (I&I) Cleaning, Personal Care, Healthcare, Agrochemicals, Emulsion Polymerisation, and Construction, among others.
Driven by a commitment to innovation, sustainability, and quality, KLK Tensachem employs state-of-the-art technologies to manufacture products such as low 1,4-dioxane SLES. Our operations are backed by globally recognised certifications, including RSPO SCC, ISCC PLUS, and ISO 50001.
As part of the KLK OLEO group, KLK Tensachem has received substantial investments to expand capabilities and reinforce its position as a major anionic surfactant producer in Europe. Our next phase of growth includes strategic investments in Alpha Olefin Sulphonates (AOS) production.
In addition to our core portfolio, KLK Tensachem offers custom manufacturing and tolling services, tailored to meet specific customer formulations and production needs.
Partner with KLK Tensachem — where expertise meets reliability in delivering advanced surfactant solutions for tomorrow’s needs.

# Palm-Oleo (Klang) Sdn. Bhd.
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Palm-Oleo (Klang) Sdn. Bhd.
In September 2007, KLK acquired Uniqema (M) Sdn. Bhd. (“UMSB”). The UMSB manufacturing facility was one of the earliest oleochemical plants in Malaysia, under the management of its previous owners Unilever and ICI/Uniqema/Croda. UMSB was subsequently renamed Palm-Oleo (Klang) Sdn. Bhd. (“POK”).
POK continues to manufacture its current range of products. The four key products are [PALMERA Fatty Acids](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-fatty-acids/) and [Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-glycerine/), [PALMOSALT Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/palmosalt-soap-download/palmosalt-soap-noodles/) and [PALMESTER Fatty Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/). POK offers consistent, high quality [PALMOSALT Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/palmosalt-soap-download/palmosalt-soap-noodles/) through the neutralisation of distilled fatty acid under advanced process control. The plant is able to customise its soap noodle composition as per customer requirements and by employing innovative solutions via its state-of-the-art soap technical centre, is able to produce specialty soap bases. [PALMESTER Fatty Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/), which range from mono-esters to polyol-esters are used in a wide range of applications such as industrial lubricants, textiles, cleaning, cosmetics, pharmaceuticals, polymers, food, paint, inks etc. to provide surfactancy, solvency and lubricity.
KLK OLEO’s R&D Centre is set up in POK as an international research, development and technology hub to service and support its global oleochemical business. The R&D team is specialised in organic synthesis, spearheading advanced analytical support, and driving the innovation of high-value applications for oleochemical derivatives. Through the establishment of this R&D Centre, the KLK OLEO Group has been able to leverage on partner relationships on numerous collaboration projects.
![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/Palm-Oleo-K.jpg)  
  
**Palm-Oleo (Klang) Sdn Bhd**  
Lot 1 & 2, Solok Waja 3, Bukit Raja Industrial Estate,  
P.O Box 83, 41070 Klang, Selangor Darul Ehsan, Malaysia. 
Tel +603 3341 2115

#### Palm-Oleo Sdn. Bhd. - KLK OLEO

# Palm-Oleo Sdn. Bhd.
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Palm-Oleo Sdn. Bhd.
A joint venture between KLK, Mitsui & Co., Ltd., Miyoshi Oil & Fat Co., Ltd. and Asahi Denka Kyogo K.K., Palm-Oleo Sdn Bhd is one of the earliest resource-based manufacturing investments by KLK. It is one of the world’s largest standalone Oleochemical producers. In 2021, KSP Manufacturing Sdn Bhd and Palmamide Sdn Bhd’s operations are merged into Palm-Oleo. The plants produce [PALMERA Fatty Acids ](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-fatty-acids/)and [Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-glycerine/), [PALMOSALT Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmosalt-soap-noodles/), [PALMOWAX Fatty Acid Bis-Amides](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmowax-fatty-acid-bis-amides/), and a wide range of [PALMOCOL Alkanolamides](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmocol-alkanolamides/).
[PALMERA Fatty Acids ](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-fatty-acids/) is used extensively by many well-known international brands in industries such as pharmaceuticals, soap, and detergents cosmetics, food emulsifiers, varnishes and paints. Fatty Acids form the critical mass of many everyday products globally. [PALMERA Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-glycerine/) also finds broad appeal in many industries due to its outstanding stability and quality consistency, crucial criteria for many of today’s products.
The natural [PALMOSALT Soap Noodles or Soap Bases](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmosalt-soap-noodles/) are produced utilising the fresh supplies of distilled Fatty Acids. [PALMOSALT](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmosalt-soap-noodles/) is widely sought by international luxury brands. It is biodegradable, 100% vegetable-based and produced from readily renewable and sustainable natural resources. Among the crucial winning merits of are its high degree of whiteness and purity and its excellent lathering properties, good skin feel and high perfume retention qualities.
[PALMOWAX Fatty Acid Bis-Amides](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmowax-fatty-acid-bis-amides/) (also known as EBS) is an excellent internal and external lubricant for thermoplastic and thermoset systems due to its much higher melting point than other primary amides. EBS functions as slip additive and releasing agent in moulded engineering metal parts. In masterbatch production, EBS is incorporated for added-value lubricating resin performance.
As nonionic secondary surfactants, [PALMOCOL Alkanolamides](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmocol-alkanolamides/) have been widely used for years in many liquid cleaning formulations. [PALMOCOL Alkanolamides](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmocol-alkanolamides/) are the preferred choice due to its nitrosamine-free feature resulting from our superior process technology. [PALMOCOL](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmocol-alkanolamides/) is an excellent thickener and foam booster in personal care, cosmetic and home care products.
Palm-Oleo’s superior product quality is guaranteed by a team of professionals working in an environment [certified](https://www.klkoleo.com/accreditations/) Halal, GMP, GMP+B2, HACCP, BRC Food, ISO 9001:2008, ISO 14001:2004 and ISO 45001:2018 standards.
![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/12/2021-POR.png)  
  
**Palm-Oleo Sdn Bhd**  
Lot 1245, Kundang Industrial Estate,  
48020 Rawang, Selangor Darul Ehsan, Malaysia. 
Tel +603 6034 4800/ 1213

# PT Perindustrian Sawit Synergi
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » PT Perindustrian Sawit Synergi
PT Perindustrian Sawit Synergi (PT PSS) is a refinery and Oleochemical complex located in East Kalimantan. It supports KLK Group’s efforts to grow its presence globally and strengthen its position as a fully integrated player in the palm oil industry.
Perindustrian Sawit Synergi includes essential facilities such as a jetty, power plant, refinery, and oleochemical plant to assist the Group’s operations in the region. Oleochemical raw materials are supplied by the company and exported to KLK OLEO’s other operating facilities for further processing into products such as [Fatty Acids](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmera-fatty-acids/), [Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmera-glycerine/), [Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmosalt-soap-noodles/), Triacetin, and [Fatty Acid Esters](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/).
![PT Perindustrian Sawit Synergi ](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/DJI_20250204101434_0965_D-2-1024x576.jpg)

#### Stolthaven (Westport) Sdn Bhd - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/stolthavenwestport/

# Stolthaven (Westport) Sdn Bhd
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Stolthaven (Westport) Sdn Bhd
Established in a joint venture between KLK Berhad and Stolthaven in 2005, Stolthaven (Westport) Sdn Bhd is a storage terminal operator providing offering tailored storage solutions to a range of products including Chemical Solvents, Inorganic Products, Petroleum Products, Veg-Oil Products, Oleochemicals, Biofuel Feedstocks, and Advanced Biodiesel.
The terminal is equipped with a variety of tanks constructed from Mild Carbon Steel, Epoxy Coated Mild Carbon Steel, and Stainless Steel, providing flexible and secure storage options to suit diverse customer requirements.
Strategically situated just two kilometres from the Westport Container Terminal, Stolthaven Westport is instrumental in optimising oleochemical supply chains through integrated storage, handling, and distribution services. With a strong emphasis on safety, operational excellence, and adaptability to market dynamics, the terminal supports clients in maintaining seamless supply flows across the region.
Stolthaven Westport is both ISCC EU and ISCC Plus certified.
![Stolthaven \(Westport\) Sdn Bhd](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/Aerial-top-T1-1024x576.jpg)
**Stolthaven (Westport) Sdn Bhd**  
Petrochemical Jetty, Westport 42009,  
Port Klang, Selangor, Malaysia

#### Supply Chain Management - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/supply-chain-management/

# Supply Chain Management
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Supply Chain Management
### Supply Chain Management in KLK OLEO
Sustainability practices are the core values of KLK OLEO’s corporate culture. These values are incorporated into an extensive self-regulating practice which integrates the principles of sustainability into a stringent supply chain management. With the surge in world demand for environmental protection, high awareness for downstream compliance to sustainability regulations and standards is fast becoming a necessity.
One of the overarching goals of KLK Sustainability Policy is to reduce and eventually end deforestation and peatland conversion associated with KLK palm oil supply chain. KLK OLEO is managing these risks by employing geospatial monitoring tools and satellite imagery to track land use changes, ensuring that all palm oil sources are free from deforestation and peatland development. These technologies enable monitoring and rapid identification of any non-compliant areas, allowing for corrective action.
![2025 04 KLK OLEO Website Sustainability Page Visual R1](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/2025-04-KLK-OLEO-Website-Sustainability-Page-Visual-R1.png)
######  For more information, please refer to [KLK website](https://www.klk.com.my/sustainability/home2023/).

#### Sustainability Principled by Integrity - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/sustainability-principled-by-integrity/

# Sustainability Principled by Integrity
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Sustainability Principled by Integrity
### Integrated, Reliable and Sustainable
KLK and its subsidiaries including KLK OLEO are committed to protecting all its resources and incorporating environmentally friendly methods into its practices policies.
At KLK OLEO, our principles on sustainability are guided by integrity.
A strong sense of community is important to us. KLK policy reaches deep into the social welfare of its communities, through the provision of education and accessible health care.
No deforestation and no new development in peat areas are the fundamental commitment from us that all oleochemicals produced are sustainable.
![EN Sustainability Principled by Integrity Chart](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/EN-Sustainability-Principled-by-Integrity-Chart-01.png)
After years of experience in multisite RSPO Supply Chain Certification since 2012, KLK OLEO is now having 14 manufacturing facilities certified, strategically located in Malaysia, Indonesia, China and Europe. Our capability in delivering Traceable, Segregated and Mass Balance certified oleo-based products enable KLK OLEO continues to grow as the most trusted global partner in oleo-based products and solutions.
Environmental management such as energy consumption, GHG emissions, water consumption, and solid waste generation are all closely monitored at sustainable levels.
KLK OLEO Sustainable Practices are recognised by world standards, and is an exemplary model to sustain the ecological balance of the environment, during the production and manufacturing of oleochemicals produced.
KLK OLEO. Trusted to enrich the global population with sustainability.
### Documents
  * ###### Sustainability Policy
[ View & Download ](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/KLK-Sustainability-Policy-EN.pdf)
  * ###### Sustainability Report
[ View & Download ](https://www.klk.com.my/sustainability/market-place/sustainability-policy-and-reports/)

### Traceability at KLK OLEO
All sustainable produce must be traceable to its source of origin. Oleochemicals produced from KLK OLEO has a highly traceable palm oil supply chain which supports long-term ecological balance of the environment.  
  

View More
![KLK OLEO Traceability English](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/2024-02-26-Link-Tracebility-English.png)  

###### Notes:
Defined as traceable to Palm Oil Mills (“POM”) level for both Palm Oil based and Palm Kernel Oil based feedstock, excluding internal transfer within KLK OLEO group of companies.
The percentages disclosed were tabulated based on the weighted average purchases for palm oil related origin processed in participating operating centers.
The source of information shared, non-verified, included but not limited to:
  * Supplier specific traceability disclosure: Suppliers submitted traceability information upon request for certain period*, based on cloud of mills. Traceability percentage is calculated based on mills information provided. Should suppliers provide statements of certain percentage traceable to POM level, this was also used to represent traceability.
  * Supplier website traceability disclosure*: Information from supplier traceability dashboard.
  * CIF Rotterdam Traceability (CRT) Template**: Specific for Europe operating centers, CRT templates ex-vessel were used as source of information for traceability calculation.
  * RSPO certified purchases: Declaration of mills by suppliers in the RSPO PalmTrace and Prisma. Despite that, according to RSPO, the mill list is not fully available in the transaction records within Prisma, as the historical footprint from PalmTrace has not yet been migrated. The information will be updated as soon as it becomes available.
  * Mill list is updated to reflect latest sourcing whenever information is available.

  * *Traceability information not necessarily match sourcing period. Only RSPO SG model can provide exact matching of traceability against sourcing period.
  * ** Typically connect to shipments over the past 12 months. Suppliers that not in current supply chain, can therefore still appear on the mill list.

### Supply Chain Management in KLK OLEO
Sustainability practices are the core values of KLK OLEO’s corporate culture. These values are incorporated into an extensive self-regulating practice which integrates the principles of sustainability into a stringent supply chain management. With the surge in world demand for environmental protection, high awareness for downstream compliance to sustainability regulations and standards is fast becoming a necessity.
One of the overarching goals of KLK Sustainability Policy is to reduce and eventually end deforestation and peatland conversion associated with KLK palm oil supply chain. KLK OLEO is managing these risks by employing geospatial monitoring tools and satellite imagery to track land use changes, ensuring that all palm oil sources are free from deforestation and peatland development. These technologies enable monitoring and rapid identification of any non-compliant areas, allowing for corrective action.
![EN Sustainability Geospatial Monitoring Chart](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/2025-04-KLK-OLEO-Website-Sustainability-Page-Visual-R1.png)

#### Taiko Palm-Oleo (Zhangjiagang) Co. Ltd. - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/taiko-palm-oleo-zhangjiagang-co-ltd/

# Taiko Palm-Oleo (Zhangjiagang) Co. Ltd.
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » Taiko Palm-Oleo (Zhangjiagang) Co. Ltd.
Taiko Palm-Oleo (Zhangjiagang) Co., Ltd. (“TPO”) was incorporated in January 2004 and operates an on-site plant at Zhangjiagang, China, manufacturing basic and intermediate oleochemicals. The plant, which was commissioned in 2006, produces [PALMERA Fatty Acids](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-fatty-acids/) and [Glycerine](https://www.klkoleo.com/our-product/palmera-glycerine/), [PALMOSALT Soap Noodles](https://www.klkoleo.com/palmosalt-soap-download/palmosalt-soap-noodles/), and [PALMESTER Triacetin](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/). It is part of KLK OLEO’s global strategy to move into the fast growing Chinese market with the overall aim of enhancing its supply, distribution and services to its customers. The Zhangjiagang main warehouse operates as TPO’s hub in China and is supported by strategic branches and warehousing facilities at various locations in China, namely Shanghai, Guangzhou, Tianjin and Nanjing; each serving customers of a specific region.
![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/Companies-TPOZ-01.jpg)

# ENHEX Glycerin Pharma Grade
![this is an image of a girl that is using eye drops, which has pharmaceutical-grade glycerine that acts as a non toxic and odourless lubricant.](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/EDENOR-Glycerine-Pharma-Grade.jpg)
###### Description:
EDENOR Glycerine is a pharmaceutical excipient. It is 100% natural and derived from renewable resources. EDENOR Glycerine is a non-toxic, hygroscopic, colourless, odourless and sweet-tasting viscous liquid. It is a good solubiliser, lubricant, plasticiser and agent for moisturising and preserving purposes.
Pharma Grade
###### Pharma Grade
  * **Product code:** G 86 PH, G 99.8 PH
  * **Application:** As a solubiliser, lubricant, plasticiser, moisturising and preserving agent in many topical and systemic medications such as capsules, cough syrups, ear drops, emulsions, enzymes, eye drops, inhalation aerosols, injections, lozenges, medical chewing gums, ointments, parenteral nutrition, suppositories, etc.

# PALMERA Fatty Acids
![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/PALMERA-Fatty-Acids01.jpg)
###### Description:
PALMERA Fatty Acids, our range of renewable products obtained from vegetable oils through classical oleochemical production processes including distillation and fractionation. Within this range of products we also offer derivatives such as dimer acids, monomer acids, trimer acids and isostearic acids.
PALMERA Fatty Acids are being applied for beauty and personal care products, lubricants, metal working, paints, inks, crayons, candles, products for Industries & Institutional (I&I) cleaning, herbicides, biocides, plastics, rubber, textiles and detergents.
Manufactured in accordance with legal requirements and quality standards such as GMP+, HACCP and ECOCERT COSMOS, a number of PALMERA Fatty Acids has been made suitable for food, feed, pharmaceutical and personal care applications.
Fractionated Fatty Acids
###### Caproic Acid
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Malaysia, China, Europe
  * **Product code:** A9806, A9906
  * **Applications:** As a flavouring and perfuming agent; Used in production of metalworking fluid to enhance cutting capabilities, grinding capabilities, and rust resistance; Used in production of specialty soaps; As a chain terminator in the production of polyester plasticiser, etc.

###### Caprylic Acid, Capric Acid, Caprylic-Capric Acid
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Malaysia, China, Europe
  * **Product code:** A5608, A9808, A9908, A9810, A9910
  * **Applications:** Used in the production of alkyl chlorides for agricultural products, methyl caprylate/caprate, fatty alcohols, acid chlorides, fatty acid isethionates, metallic soaps, fatty acid sarcosinates, imidazolines, fatty amines, MCT oils, and polyol esters as lubricant in metalworking and as emollient in personal care products, and oxazolines for paint binder ; Used in herbicide, animal feed, corrosion/rust inhibitors for antifreeze, flotation agents in mining, creams and lotion formulations, plasticisers, etc.

###### Lauric Acid
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Malaysia, China, Europe
  * **Product code:** A4512, A7012, A9212, A9812, A9912
  * **Applications:** Used in the production of various esters, fatty alcohols, fatty acid isethionates, metallic soaps, fatty acid sarcosinates, imidazolines, fatty amines, oxazolines for paint binder, surfactants in cosmetics, liquid and transparent soaps; Used in agricultural chemicals, food, etc.

###### Myristic Acid
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Malaysia, China, Europe
  * **Product code:** A9814, A9914
  * **Applications:** Used in the production of various esters, fatty alcohols, fatty acid isethionates, metallic soaps, fatty acid sarcosinates, imidazolines, fatty amines, oxazolines for paint binder, surfactants in cosmetics, liquid and transparent soaps; Used in agricultural chemicals, food, etc.

###### Palmitic Acid
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Malaysia, China, Europe

  * **Applications:** Used in the production of various esters, fatty alcohols, fatty acid isethionates, metallic soaps, fatty acid sarcosinates, imidazolines, fatty amines, oxazolines for paint binder, surfactants in personal care products, liquid and transparent soaps, corrosion/rust inhibitor for antifreeze; Used in agricultural chemicals, food, adhesive, crayon, candles, cements, coatings, inks, leather waxes, lubricants, metalworking, mining, pencils, capsules and ointments, plastics, rubber, textiles, etc.

###### Stearic Acid

  * **Applications:** Used in the production of various esters, fatty alcohols, fatty acid isethionates, metallic soaps, fatty acid sarcosinates, imidazolines, fatty amines, oxazolines for paint binder, surfactants in personal care products, liquid and transparent soaps, corrosion/rust inhibitor for antifreeze; Used in agricultural chemicals, food, adhesive, crayon, candles, cements, coatings, inks, leather waxes, lubricants, metalworking, mining, pencils, capsules and ointments, plastics, rubber, textiles, etc.

#### PALMERA Refined Glycerine | KLK OLEO Malaysia

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmera-glycerine/

Products
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » [Brands](https://www.klkoleo.com/de/brand/) » PALMERA Glycerine
# PALMERA Glycerine
![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Refined20Glycerine.jpg)
###### Description:
PALMERA Refined Glycerine is a high‑purity, colourless, odourless, hygroscopic, and viscous liquid derived from vegetable oils through classical oleochemical processes, including splitting and distillation.
Known for its versatility and quality, PALMERA Refined Glycerine is widely used across various industries—tobacco products, surface coatings, paper, inks, lubricants, textiles, urethane polymers, and more. It also serves as a key raw material for producing value‑added chemicals such as propylene glycol, epichlorohydrin, acrolein, and nitroglycerine.
Manufactured in compliance with stringent legal and quality standards, including GMP+ and HACCP, several PALMERA Refined Glycerine grades are suitable for use in food, flavours, feed, and personal care applications. Products meeting the specifications of relevant pharmacopoeias are also available for pharmaceutical use.
Refined Glycerine
###### British Pharmacopeia (BP)
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Malaysia, China
  * **Product code:** G995B
  * **Applications:** Used in pharmaceuticals, personal care product, toiletries and cosmetics as solvents and humectants; Food additives; Animal feed; As humidifiers and plasticisers for tobacco.

![fatty alcohols is a raw materials used for surface cleaners, disinfectants and more](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Fatty20Alcohols.jpg)
###### Description:
PALMEROL Fatty Alcohols are high-quality fatty alcohols derived from vegetable oils and are available in various fractions. These fatty alcohols play a vital role in the production of household detergents, with mid-cut and long-chain types being the most in demand. The primary application of natural fatty alcohols lies in their conversion into derivatives for use in surfactants and detergent formulations.

# PALMESTER Fatty Acid Esters
![Cetyl ethylhexanoate is used in beauty and personal care that the women is looking at](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/PALMESTER-Fatty-Acid-Esters.jpg)
###### Description:
PALMESTER Fatty Acid Esters are used in a broad variety of different industrial applications such as cosmetics, toiletries, pharmaceuticals, food, metal working fluids, and etc.
Properties like lubrication, mould release, consistency promotion, emolliency, stability enhancement, spreading or fatty, solvency and cleaning demonstrate the large variety of functions offered by these unique products.
Branched Fatty Esters
###### Cetyl Ethylhexanoate
  * **Applications:** Used in beauty & personal care. Vegetable-base, non-oily with a light silky skin after-feel.

###### Glyceryl 2-ethylhexanoate
  * **Applications:** Used in beauty & personal care. Vegetable-base and provides a non-greasy and cushiony feel.

Butyl Esters
###### N-Butyl Stearate (nBS)
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Flow promoter/plasticizer for injection molding grades of polystyrene; Lubricant in processing of synthetic fibers; Metalworking lubricant in rolling of aluminum.

Butylene Glycol Esters
###### Butylene Glycol Dicaprylate/Dicaprate
  * **Applications:** An emollient and skin conditioning agent for beauty and personal care products.

Ethylhexyl Esters
###### Ethylhexyl Cocoate (EHC)
  * **Applications:** Fatty acid ester derived from coconut fat with softening properties, non-occlusive and fine emollient.

###### Ethylhexyl Oleate (EHO)
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Solvent for antibiotics and vitamins; After epoxidation as low temperature plasticizer for PVC; After sulphurisation as high pressure additive for lubricants.

###### Ethylhexyl Stearate (EHS)
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Offers good lubricity and low volatility as component of specialized textile fiber dressings; Can be used in carbon stencil manufacture and pigment carrier in printing inks; Better clean burn and minimal stain for cold rolling than tallow.

###### Ethylhexyl Palmitate (EHP)
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Can be used to replace mineral oil; For night cream, hand cream, cleansing lotion, baby cream & massage lotion.

Glyceryl Esters
###### Triacetin
  * **Applications:** Plasticizer for cigarette filters; Solvent for flavorings; General purpose food additive; Ester catalyst/curing agent in sand casting; Raw material for food ester (e.g. Acetylated Monoglycerides); Plasticizer for adhesive, and etc.

###### Glycerol Trioleate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Lubricant component of synthetic fibre finishes. Replacement of a variety of natural oils. Synthetic oil for fat liquor production.

###### Caprylic / Capric Triglyceride
  * **Applications:** Caprylic Capric Triglyceride (also known as MCT oils) can be used for animal feed and agricultural application; Inert carrier for flavor, oil soluble food colorants, fat soluble vitamins and fat soluble additives. Manufactured to the best bland taste for flavoring agents, to ensure excellent keeping qualities and compatibility with sensitive flavors. Superb colorless properties for transparency and enhancing natural color; Miscible in more polar solvents such as alcohols, even in the presence of water; Good spreading properties for bakeries coating, mould release and lubricating production transport system; The superb color & odor makes it a good emollient for cosmetics & toiletries; Standard grade, produced to colorless, odorless and bland taste for food applications.

Glycol Esters
###### Glycol Stearate
  * **Applications:** Pearlescing agent.

###### Glycol Distearate
  * **Applications:** Pearlescing agent.

Isopropyl Esters
###### Isopropyl Oleate (IPO)
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Hydrolytically stable, medium oxidative stability; Can be used in concrete waterproofing agent.

###### Isopropyl Laurate (IPL)
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Use in ink industries.

###### Isopropyl Palmitate (IPP)
  * **Applications:** The standard polar emollient for cosmetic and toiletries use.

###### Isopropyl Myristate (IPM)
  * **Applications:** The polar emollient produced to stringent odor and color standards special for fragrance use and for excellent keeping qualities; As biolubricant base oil; Can be used in metal working.

###### Isopropyl Stearate (IPS)
  * **Applications:** A fast spreading emollient suitable for all beauty and cosmetics applications.

Isostearates
###### Isopropyl Isostearate (IPIS)
  * **Applications:** Ideal for heat sensitive personal care formulations. It enables superior odour and colour end-products. It also exhibits excellent skin feel without feel of greasiness. It is widely used in colour cosmetics, bath oil, skin and hair care.

###### Pentaerytritol Tetraisostearate (PTIS)
  * **Applications:** Ideal for heat sensitive personal care formulations. It enables superior odour and colour end-products. It also exhibits excellent skin feel without feel of greasiness. It is widely used in colour cosmetics, bath oil, skin and hair care.

Methyl Esters
###### Methyl Laurate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Lubricity additive in the production of aluminum metal working fluids; Paint and ink additives.

Neopentyl Glycol Esters
###### Neopentyl Glycol Dioleate
  * **Applications:** Used as biolubricant base oil and additive for metal working fluid and rolling oil.

Pentaerythritol Esters
###### Pentaerythritol Tetracaprylate/Tetracaprate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Used in metal working & industrial chain & greases; Ideal lubricant component for spin finish; Lubricity additive in formulation of high temperature metalworking fluids; Used in hair conditioners, cream and lotions, sunscreens and bath oil with functional properties of skin conditioning agent, occlusive agent and emollient.

###### Pentaerythritol Tetraoleate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; in formulation of high temperature metal working fluids.

###### Pentaerythritol Tetrastearate (PETS)
  * **Applications:** PETS is typical used as internal and external lubricant for plastic industry. It is well match with polycarbonate, but also in PA, PET and other plastics. Besides, PETS also function as mould release agent with low volatility and suitable for high transparent product. Dosage recommendation up to 0.5% in plastic article is able to remain product transparent property and colour stability.

Propylene Glycol Esters
###### Propylene Glycol Dicaprylate/Dicaprate
  * **Applications:** Solvent, vehicle, diluent, carrier for flavors and fragrance oils, colorants, vitamines, medicinals, cosmetics; Emollients for cosmetics, pharmaceuticals, creams/lotions; Food additives; Used in resinous/polymeric food-contact coatings; Defoamer in food-contact paper/paperboard; As biolubricant base oil.

###### Propylene Glycol Dioleate

Trimethylolpropane Esters
###### Trimethylolpropane Caprate/Caprylate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Used in metal working, automotive, engine oils & industrial air compressor; Good thermal and oxidative stability.

###### Trimethylolpropane Trioleate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Used in fire resistant & biodegradable hydraulic fluids and metal working. Low pour point with excellent lubricity & oxidation stability. High flash point hydraulic fluids.

###### Trimethylolpropane Palm Cocoate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Used in metal working, as rolling fluids and textile lubricants; Good clean burn properties and enhanced lubricity for high temperature applications.

###### Trimethylolpropane Palm Kernelate
  * **Applications:** As biolubricant base oil; Used in metal working, as rolling fluids and textile lubricants; Good clean burn properties and enhanced lubricity for high temperature applications.

Wax Esters
###### Cetyl Palmitate
  * **Applications:** An emollient that lubricates and conditions the skin’s surface, helping it to appear softer and smoother. It is also used as a surfactant in shampoos, and as an emulsifier and thickening agent in various lotions and creams. It helps add texture various makeup products. Great skin-conditioning ingredient, also used as occlusive and masking ingredient, enhances emulsion stability, excellent film former, reduces greasy feel of oil systems, improves payoff characteristics in stick cosmetics.

###### Decyl Oleate
  * **Applications:** Used in a variety of cosmetics and personal care products, including makeup, and skin and hair care products; Acts as a lubricant on the skin surface, which gives the skin a soft and smooth appearance It forms a thin film on the skin that is neither greasy nor tacky. Its unique properties facilities the application and removal of makeup.

Products
[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » [Brands](https://www.klkoleo.com/de/brand/) » PALMFONATE Methyl Ester Sulphonates
# PALMFONATE Methyl Ester Sulphonates
![this is an image of a girl helping out with the laundry, and the active cleaning ingredients is Methyl Ester Sulphonate](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/Sulphonated20Methyl20Esters.jpg)
###### Description:
PALMFONATE Methyl Ester Sulphonates (MES) is KLK OLEO’s proprietary brand of anionic surfactant, widely recognised as the active cleaning ingredient in laundry detergents. Derived from Methyl Esters of natural fatty acids, this MES surfactant serves as a high-performing, eco-friendly alternative to traditional surfactants like Linear Alkyl Benzene Sulfonate (LABS).
Formulated from renewable natural resources, PALMFONATE offers superior detergency, excellent biodegradability, and enhanced calcium hardness tolerance during washing. These qualities make it ideal for both high-efficiency and conventional cleaning applications.
PALMFONATE MES is available in multiple formats—free-flowing powder, flakes, and paste—offering formulation flexibility. The powder grade allows for easy, direct post-addition into detergent formulas during manufacturing.

# PALMOCOL Alkanolamides
![KLK OLEO PALMOCOL Alkanolamides](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/PALMOCOL-Alkanolamides-1.jpg)
###### Description:
PALMOCOL Alkanolamides are high purity ingredients for application in toiletries, liquid detergents and personal care products like shampoos, bubble baths, hand washers, body and facial cleansers. PALMOCOL Alkanolamides include Methyl Monotheanolamides, Monoethanolamides and Diethanolamides, which are excellent in building viscosity; boosting and stabilising foams – making it suitable for use in anionic or nonionic surfactants. The Monoethanolamide grade can be used in various tablet formulations, such as synthetic soap bars, to effectively control its dissolution rate.
Methyl Monoethanolamides
###### Palm Kernelamide Methyl MEA / Cocamide Methyl MEA
  * **Product code:** PMMA 1300, PMMA 2300
  * **Applications:** Excellent alternative product of cocamide diethanolamides (CDEA) and cocamide monoethanolamide (CMEA). It shows superior performance in foam speed and better thickening effect in shampoo, body wash, hand wash and other liquid detergents.

Diethanolamides
###### Palm Kernelamide DEA / Cocamide DEA
  * **Product code:** PDA 1100, PDA 1300, PDA 1320, PDA 2300
  * **Applications:** Excellent viscosity builder, foam boosters & foam stabilizers for personal wash formulation especially in anionic based cleansers like shampoos, hand washer and body cleansers; Emulsifiers and solubilizing agents in facial cleansing cream; Also used in industrial cleaners and dish washes.

Monoethanolamides
###### Palm Kernelamide MEA / Cocamide MEA
  * **Product code:** PMA 1100, PMA 1300, PMA 2300
  * **Applications:** Excellent viscosity builder, foam boosters & foam stabilizers for personal wash formulation especially in anionic based cleansers like shampoos, hand washer and body cleansers; Emulsifiers and solubilizing agents in facial cleansing cream; Used in various tablet formulation, e.g. synthetic soap bar to effectively control its dissolution rate.

# PALMOSALT Soap Noodles
![this is an image of a mother and a baby showering using PALMOSALT soap noodle.](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/PALMOSALT20Soap20Noodles20base.jpg)
###### Description:
KLK OLEO offers a wide range of soap compositions for different formulations and quality aspects. Due to its wide-ranging versatility and overall properties, PALMOSALT range of Soap Noodles meets the varied needs of our clients in the cosmetic, toiletry and laundry detergent manufacturing industries.
HIGH LOADING Soap Noodle is a versatile base for dense, creamy and silky lather bar soap. It possesses excellent additive high-loading properties; for example, greater perfume leading results in better perfume retention and subsequently continued release. As it is versatile & specialised in usage, the base offers flexibility for cosmetic & toiletry companies launching new varieties of bar soap trend products in the market.
NOMAR Soap Noodle was developed to enhance the look and condition of complex soap shapes and patterning. Its excellent processing characteristics and properties enable the production and easy manufacture of intricate shapes in soap bars.

# PALMOWAX Fatty Acid Bis-Amides
![Fatty acid amides includes oleyl palmitatmide, EBS wax that is used as anti block agents in plastic coatings, inks and rubber.](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/PALMOWAX-Fatty-Acid-Bis-Amides.jpg)
###### Description:
KLK OLEO’s range of **fatty acid amides** includes high-purity ingredients such as **Oleyl Palmitamide** , **Ethylene Bis-Stearamide (EBS)** , and **Ethylene Bis-Oleamide (EBO)** under the **PALMOWAX** brand. These versatile compounds serve as slip agents, dispersants, and anti-block agents across multiple industries including plastics, coatings, inks, and rubber.
As a trusted **EBS supplier** , we provide **EBS wax** and **EBO** in forms suitable for both internal and external lubrication of plastics such as ABS, PS, and PP. **PALMOWAX** also functions as a binder in precision metal engineering applications. 
Its excellent surface migration and dispersion properties make it valuable in ink formulations, while its addition to asphalt enhances viscoelasticity and raises the softening point—ideal for road construction.
For even greater formulation flexibility, these **fatty acid amides** can be paired with our **polymer additives** or[ **fatty acid esters**](https://www.klkoleo.com/brand/palmester-fatty-acid-esters/) to enhance thermal stability and processing efficiency in complex systems.
Ethylene Bis-Stearamide
###### Ethylene Bis-Stearamide (EBS)
  * **Product code:**
Atomised; 1% ≤ on 325 mesh  
Bead; 15% ≤ on 10 mesh  
SF; 85% ≥ pass 200 mesh  
SP; 90% ≥ pass 16 mesh  
SR; 90% ≥ pass 16 mesh
  * **Applications:** EBS is widely used in plastic industry as internal/external lubricants, mold releasing aids, dispersants and slip agents; As dispersants in printing inks; Increase softening point in asphalt modifiers; As lubricants in powder metal molding, rubber, adhesive, coatings, wire drawing, wood plastic composite; As defoamers in paper.

Ethylene Bis-Oleamide
###### Ethylene Bis-Oleamide (EBO)
  * **Product code:**
SPO; 90% ≥ pass 16 mesh
  * **Applications:** EBO is an excellent lubricant in plastic Applicationss especially in styrenic polymer such as PS and ABS to help with polymer flow, mould release and anti-caking properties of the polymer; Also widely used as anti-blocking agents in PVC film and sheet.

# Sympare Methyl Ester Sulphonates
![this is an image of a girl washing her hands using a hand soap that uses sympare MES that is a sustainable ingredient for personal care.](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/SYMAPRE-MES-01.jpg)
###### Description:
Sympare Methyl Ester Sulphonates (MES) is a sulphate-free anionic surfactant naturally made from plant source for use in the personal care categories. Sympare MES is listed in the CleanGredients database as it meets the EPA’s Safer Choice Standard. It is a sustainable alternative to petroleum-based surfactants in cleansing product formulations where product’s low irritation and mildness are benefits.
Sympare MES contains more than 50% of natural raw materials and is defined as derived natural ingredient according to ISO 16128. It is a suitable alternative ingredient recommended for high-growth natural consumer product categories such as baby care, personal care, and cosmetics.

# TEMEST Esters
![TEMEST Ester 3](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/03/TEMEST-Ester-3.png)
###### Description:
TEMEST Esters represent KLK OLEO’s comprehensive portfolio of high‑performance ester‑based lubricants designed to meet the evolving needs of modern industries. Developed from renewable raw materials, the TEMEST range delivers exceptional lubricity, oxidative stability, biodegradability, and versatility across applications such as metalworking fluids, hydraulics, gears, compressors, automotive, food‑grade lubricants, and more. From mono esters to advanced polyol and polymeric esters, each product is engineered to enhance efficiency, reduce wear, and support sustainable, reliable performance in demanding operating environments.
Mono Esters
TEMEST Mono Esters are a specialised class of high‑performance ester lubricants, produced through the reaction of a monocarboxylic acid with a monohydric alcohol. Engineered for versatility and reliability, they offer excellent lubricity, stability, and functionality across a wide spectrum of formulations.
At KLK OLEO, we provide a comprehensive portfolio of premium Mono Esters, each meticulously developed to deliver consistent quality and optimised performance for diverse industrial and cosmetic applications.

Aromatic Esters
Aromatic esters belong to a premium category of ester lubricants, engineered from the combination of an aromatic acid and a monohydric alcohol.
This family of aromatic is ideal for high-temperature applications such as chain oils and greases in higher viscosity grades. Their outstanding cold properties also make them an excellent choice for compressor and refrigeration applications.

Unsaturated Polyol Esters
Unsaturated Polyol Esters are produced through the reaction of polyols and unsaturated fatty acids. KLK OLEO offers a wide range of viscosities, from ISO 15 to 68, to meet various lubrication needs.
These products feature high viscosity indexis (VIs) and flash points, along with rapid biodegradability, making them a perfect choice for Environmentally Acceptable Lubricants (EALS) such as hydraulic fluids (HFDU and HEES).

Polymeric Esters
Polymeric Esters are a specialised class of ester lubricants, characterised by their high molecular weight, typically above ISO 100. KLK OLEO offers a wide range of products, divided into two sub-families: unsaturated and saturated.
These esters can be utilised as base oils (over 85%), co-base stocks (10-50%), or additives (less than 10%) to enhance physical-chemical properties and tribological performance.
When operating in the elasto-hydrodynamic range, they optimise the film layer, resulting in higher energy efficiency and improved overall performance.
###### TEMEST H65100
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Europe
  * **Properties/Features:** Thanks to their film-forming capabilities, they create a protective layer that reduces the coefficient of friction in Boundary and Mixed lubrication conditions, helping to protect against wear and EP failures.
  * **Applications:** Hydraulics and greases.

TEMEST FC Esters
TEMEST FC is a family of products developed by KLK OLEO to support the formulation of food-grade lubricants for use in food processing and packaging environments. The range offers customers a broad selection of base oils, co-base fluids, and additive components suitable for lubricant formulations intended for incidental food contact (NSF HX-1) and direct food contact (NSF 3H) applications, when formulated and used in accordance with applicable NSF requirements.
As a result, formulations based on TEMEST FC help extend maintenance intervals, reduce cleaning frequency and associated costs, enhance machinery protection, and improve overall energy efficiency, delivering long-term value and operational reliability in demanding food-processing environments. All this while offering a sustainable profile, with high renewability and biodegradability.
###### TEMEST FC 1920 / Saturated Polyol Ester
  * **Manufacturing Site:** Europe
  * **Properties/Features:** Reduced friction and wear, cleaner system operation, excellent oxidative stability, reliable high-temperature performance, and low volatility. Certified NSF HX-1 and NSF 3H.

[Home](https://www.klkoleo.com/) » [Brands](https://www.klkoleo.com/de/brand/) » TENSAGEX Alcohol Ether Sulphates
# TENSAGEX Alcohol Ether Sulphates
![fatty alcohol ether sulfate is a primary surfactant in detergent used for everyday laundry at home](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/tensagex_home20care.jpg)
###### Description:
Available in both Sodium Laureth Sulphate and Ammonium Laureth Sulphate forms, TENSAGEX products offer excellent cleansing, emulsifying, and foaming performance. As an anionic surfactant, TENSAGEX is widely used as a primary surfactant in rinse-off formulations, including personal care and household cleaning products.
Derived from natural fatty alcohols, TENSAGEX is characterised by its high purity and low levels of 1,4-dioxane, salt, and colour, aligning with current regulatory requirements across global markets.
To support customers’ sustainability goals, TENSAGEX is also available with RSPO Mass Balance (MB) certification, ensuring responsible sourcing of palm-derived ingredients.
We are also proud to announce the launch of our new sustainable ISCC+ certified Ether Sulfates, TENSAGEX MAX series. In the TENSAGEX MAX series, the ethylene oxide content is based on ethylene oxide derived from sustainable raw materials, such as recycled oils or natural oils. The ISCC+ certification ensures traceability through the Mass Balance approach.

# TENSARYL Linear Alkylbenzene Sulphonates & Sulphonic Acids
![Surfactant suppliers](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/04/tensary2004.jpg)
###### Description:
The TENSARYL product line consists of linear alkylbenzene sulphonic acids (also known as LABSA) and salts.  
LABSA and LAS are anionic surfactants that exhibit very good detergent properties for relatively low cost. For this reason, they are considered one of the main work-horses in large volume applications, such as household and industrial laundry and dishwashing detergents, and all-purpose cleaners. Further applications include textile processing and emulsifiers in industrial and agricultural applications.
Linear Alkylbenzene Sulphonic Acid (LABSA)
###### Product:
TENSARYL SBLA
  * **Properties/Features:** HF LAB, 96% active matter; Viscous liquid

  * **Applications:** Laundry, dishwashing, hard surface cleaners, detergent creams/powders, disinfectants, toilet cleaners, car cleaners, paint strippers, emulsion polymerisation.

TENSARYL SBU
  * **Properties/Features:** Detal LAB, 96% active matter; Viscous liquid; Low colour

  * **Applications:** Laundry, dishwashing, hard surface cleaners, detergent creams/powders, disinfectants, toilet cleaners, car cleaners, paint strippers, emulsion polymerisation.

#### 可持续 - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/timeline-post/%e5%8f%af%e6%8c%81%e7%bb%ad%e7%9a%84/

# 可持续
![KLK OLEO RISE](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
使用KLK OLEO产品，实现您的可持续发展目标，进一步提高您的可持续发展能力。生物活性剂、生物燃料、生物润滑剂、绿色化学品、生物塑料和生物聚合物等新应用将提高您的道德水平和环境友好度。  
我们承诺遵循“没有砍伐、没有泥炭和没有剥削”（NDPE）原则、降低温室气体（GHG）排放、参与可持续棕榈油圆桌会议（RSPO），并在整个供应链和运营过程中贯彻可持续发展政策。这一系列承诺赢得了全球顶级快速消费品公司的信任，确保我们的产品符合最严格的环境标准。

![2025 RISE Thumbnail](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/2025-RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
KLK OLEO 拥有世界一流的生产设施，这些工厂战略性地位于马来西亚、中国、印度尼西亚、欧洲，另外我们还在美洲新设立了国际销售办事处。 我们拥有一支技术型的商业团队，致力于提供市场信息、销售响应和配方支持等，旨在最大限度地提高效率并提高您的产品质量。

![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/cr-employeest-01.jpg)

# Bildung
![](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/06/cr-education-01.jpg)
Wir sind davon überzeugt, dass Bildung ein wichtiger Katalysator für positive Veränderungen ist und die Zukunft der Gemeinschaft verändert. Daher bemühen wir uns, in den Gebieten, in denen wir tätig sind, geeignete Bildungswege für Menschen und Gemeinschaften zu schaffen, die nicht das Glück haben, Zugang zu solchen Möglichkeiten zu haben.

#### Efficient - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/timeline-post/efficient/

# Efficient
![2025 RISE Thumbnail](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/2025-RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
KLK OLEO serves our customers from world-class manufacturing facilities strategically located across Malaysia, China, Indonesia, Europe, and our newly established international sales office in the Americas. With a team of techno-commercial professionals, we are committed to delivering the beneﬁts of market knowledge, sales responsiveness and formulation support, intending to maximise efficiency and improve the quality of your products.  
With a presence in every region, we can react swiftly to your demands and deliver products of the highest standard just-in-time for your production. This will reduce carbon footprint while ensuring product integrity and quality during handling and storage.

#### Effizient - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/timeline-post/effizient/

# Effizient
![2025 RISE Thumbnail](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/2025-RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
KLK OLEO bedient unsere Kunden von erstklassigen Produktionsstätten aus, die strategisch in Malaysia, China, Indonesien, Europa und unserem neu gegründeten internationalen Vertriebsbüro in Amerika liegen.  
Mit einem Team von technisch-kaufmännischen Fachleuten sind wir bestrebt, die Vorteile von Marktkenntnissen, Verkaufsreaktionen und Formulierungs-unterstützung zu liefern, um die Effizienz zu maximieren und die Qualität Ihrer Produkte zu verbessern.  
Durch Präsenz in allen Regionen können wir schnell auf Ihre Anforderungen reagieren und Produkte auf höchstem Niveau just-in-time für Ihre Produktion liefern.  
Dies wird den CO2-Fußabdruck reduzieren und gleichzeitig die Produktintegrität und -qualität während der Handhabung und Lagerung gewährleisten.

# Integriert
![2025 RISE Thumbnail](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/2025-RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)

#### Nachhaltig - KLK OLEO

- **Source:** https://www.klkoleo.com/timeline-post/nachhaltig/

# Nachhaltig
![2025 RISE Thumbnail](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/2025-RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
Erreichen Sie Ihre nachhaltigen Ziele und verbessern Sie weiter Ihr Nachhaltigkeitsprofil mit KLK OLEO Produkten.  
Neue Anwendungen wie Bioaktivstoffe, Biokraftstoffe, Bioschmierstoffe, grüne Chemikalien, Biokunststoffe und Biopolymere werden Ihre ethischen und umweltfreundlichen Voraussetzungen verbessern.  
Unser Engagement für die Themen „keine Abholzung, kein Torfabbau und keine Ausbeutung“, die Reduzierung von Treibhausgas-emissionen, unser Runder Tisch für nachhaltiges Palmöl (RSPO) und unsere Nachhaltigkeitspolitik in unserer gesamten Lieferkette und in unseren Betrieben, dem weltweit führende FMCGs vertrauen, stellt sicher, dass unsere Produkte die strengsten Umweltstandards übertreffen.

# Sustainable
![2025 RISE Thumbnail](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/2025-RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
Meet your sustainable goals and further improve your sustainability proﬁle with KLK OLEO products. New applications such as bioactives, biofuel, biolubricants, green chemicals, bioplastics, and biopolymers will increase your ethical and environmentally friendly credentials.  
Trusted by top FMCGs worldwide, our commitment to No Deforestation, No Peat, and No Exploitation (NDPE), reducing Greenhouse Gas (GHG) emissions, Roundtable on Sustainable Palm Oil (RSPO) and sustainability policy throughout our supply chain and operations ensure that our products exceed the most stringent environmental standards.

# Zuverlässig
![KLK OLEO RISE](https://www.klkoleo.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/11/RISE-Thumbnail-01.png)
Genießen Sie die innere Ruhe, indem Sie uns als Ihren wichtigsten Partner wählen.  
Mit über 100 Jahren Erfahrung bedient KLK OLEO eine Reihe von Branchen und verfügt über das Wissen und die Expertise, um Sie beim Erreichen Ihrer Produkt-entwicklungsziele zu unterstützen und gleichzeitig Leistung, Qualität und Versorgungssicherheit zu gewährleisten.  
Unsere globale Präsenz stellt sicher, dass wir alle Formulierungsziele unserer Kunden auf lokaler Ebene effektiv unterstützen können.  
Wenden Sie sich an uns, und wir werden immer alles daran setzen, ein Produkt zu entwickeln, das das Leben aller Menschen heute und morgen bereichert.

### Manual checks before launch

- Confirm which homepage slides are current; WordPress/Elementor may retain inactive banner media in the library.
- Resolve CMS taxonomy IDs to approved display labels during migration.
- Confirm expired vacancies before importing careers.
- Confirm whether historical news, reports, and timeline entries should remain published or move to an archive state.
- Run an accessibility pass on every retained image and replace blank or filename-like alt text.
- Validate document links and obtain replacement files for any protected, missing, or redirected asset.
